# Experiment summary (20261104_122025)

Median over scenes. `win` = share of scenes beating the baseline at 75-90°, `gain` = median relative AbsRel reduction with 95% bootstrap CI, `p` = paired two-sided Wilcoxon. Claim a gain only if the CI excludes 0 and p < 0.05 under both protocols, with 0-30° not worse.
